Baseline
- Previous block: P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-BD
- Previous commit: c7a6a22
- Previous message: docs: verify C8-I-D4-L-L-O-BD real auth session runtime adapter

Gate decision
PASS_REAL_AUTH_SESSION_RUNTIME_ADAPTER_CONNECTION_DESIGN_ONLY

Next step decision
P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-BF - Controlled Activity Intake Real Auth Session Runtime Adapter Connection Contract

Reason for O-BE
O-BC created serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts as a static runtime adapter boundary.
O-BD verified the static adapter and confirmed no real Auth0/session runtime reads were executed.
O-BE inspects the current project Auth0/session helper evidence with git grep only and defines the design gate for a later connection contract.
O-BE does not connect the adapter to a real Auth0/session helper.
O-BE does not call Auth0/session.
O-BE does not modify code.

Current verified state
- serverSideAuthSession.ts exists and remains static/no-I/O.
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts exists and remains static/no-I/O.
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts has auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=false.
- route.ts does not call serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts.
- route.ts remains no-write preview only.
- productionWriteEnabled=false remains in route.ts.
- no Supabase app-user mapping read is approved.
- no production activity_events insert is approved.

O-BE scope
O-BE may inspect local Auth0/session implementation evidence with git grep only.
O-BE may define the next connection contract scope.
O-BE may not implement a connection.
O-BE may not import Auth0.
O-BE may not call getSession/getAccessToken.
O-BE may not read cookies or headers.
O-BE may not modify route.ts.
O-BE may not read or write Supabase.
O-BE may not execute SQL.
O-BE may not call AI.

Connection design principle
The first real connection must remain isolated from route.ts.
The adapter connection must be introduced in a controlled helper layer first.
The route can only be integrated later after:
- connection contract
- connection verification
- no-write runtime read test design
- route integration design gate
- route integration verification

Recommended O-BF scope
O-BF should create a connection contract only.
O-BF should not implement the connection.
O-BF should not modify serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts.
O-BF should define:
- exact project Auth0 helper boundary
- required input shape
- required sanitized output shape
- required fail-closed behavior
- exact forbidden token/raw-session output rules
- exact tests for a later implementation
- whether a project-auth wrapper file is needed
- what must remain disconnected from route.ts

Allowed future connection file candidates
O-BF may choose one future file, but must not create it:
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeConnection.ts
OR a project-auth-specific adapter if current Auth0 integration clearly requires it.

Preferred future file
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeConnection.ts

Future connection helper role
The future connection helper should:
- call only the approved project Auth0/session server helper
- sanitize the result immediately
- return a sessionReadModel-compatible object
- never return tokens
- never return raw session/profile/cookies
- never query Supabase
- never create users/app users
- never integrate with route.ts directly in the same step

Future connection helper must not:
- decide appUserId
- decide actorId
- decide organizationId
- decide spaceId
- read app_users/users from Supabase
- write app_users/users to Supabase
- insert activity_events
- call AI
- derive categories or state

Required O-BF contract questions
O-BF must answer:
- Which Auth0 package/helper is currently installed and used?
- Which server-side session function is approved?
- Is the approved helper compatible with Next.js App Router?
- Does the helper require request/response objects?
- Does the helper read cookies internally?
- Can the helper be called from a server-only module?
- What fields are returned by the helper?
- Which field is the stable subject/sub?
- How are expired sessions represented?
- How are missing sessions represented?
- How are exceptions represented?
- Can the helper return tokens?
- How will token leakage be prevented?
- How will raw session leakage be prevented?
- How will email-only identity be rejected?
- How will sessionReadModel be passed into serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts?
- How will tests avoid real cookie/session access at this stage?
- What later step may execute the first real Auth0/session runtime read?

Required O-BF failure codes
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_CONNECTION_NOT_APPROVED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_CONNECTION_UNAVAILABLE
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_CONNECTION_READ_FAILED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_CONNECTION_RETURNED_UNSAFE_PAYLOAD
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SUBJECT_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_EXPIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_INVALID
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED

Required future connection guardrails
- connectionHelperOnly=true
- realAuth0SessionReadExecuted=false in contract/design/static tests
- auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=false until explicitly approved runtime test
- serverSideSessionReadOnly=true
- serverSideContextRequired=true
- clientOwnershipAccepted=false
- previewHeaderAcceptedForProductionWrite=false
- dbReadExecuted=false
- dbWriteExecuted=false
- sqlExecuted=false
- aiCallExecuted=false
- semanticCandidatesPersisted=false
- valueObjectsCreated=false
- stateFactsCreated=false
- stateDeltasCreated=false
- stateSnapshotsCreated=false

Required O-BF output contract
The contract must ensure the connection helper outputs only:
- provider: "auth0"
- authSubject
- email if available and safe
- sessionId if available and safe
- expiresAt if available and safe
- issuedAt if available and safe
- rawSessionAvailable
- readSource
- readFailed/readFailureMessage on controlled failure

Forbidden output
The connection helper must not output:
- access_token
- refresh_token
- id_token
- raw cookie
- raw session
- raw profile
- full Auth0 user object
- name
- picture
- permissions/roles unless separately approved later

Required O-BF test plan
O-BF must require later implementation tests for:
- connection not approved
- missing Auth0 helper
- missing session
- expired session
- missing sub/authSubject
- blank sub/authSubject
- unsupported provider
- unsafe payload with token fields
- raw session leakage attempt
- helper exception
- valid sanitized Auth0-like session object
- output accepted by serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts
- no Supabase read/write
- no SQL
- no AI
- route unchanged

Local evidence collection note
O-BE collected local Auth0/session evidence with git grep only.
This is not an Auth0 runtime call.
This is not a session read.
This is not a Supabase read.
This is not SQL execution.
This is not a DB write.

Evidence count
- Local Auth0/session connection evidence count: 916

Important limitation after O-BE
O-BE approves only a connection contract design step.
It does not approve:
- implementation of a real connection
- first real Auth0/session runtime read
- route integration
- Supabase app-user mapping read
- production write
- activity_events insert
- AI/semantic persistence

Still forbidden after O-BE
